const MEDIAS = [

  { key: 'バイトル',     label: 'バイトル',     match: v => ['バイトル','バイトルPro'].includes(v) },

  { key: 'マイナビ',     label: 'マイナビ',     match: v => v === 'マイナビ' },

  { key: 'e介護転職',    label: 'e介護転職',    match: v => v === 'e介護転職' },

  { key: 'キューメイト', label: 'キューメイト', match: v => ['キューメイト','indeed'].includes(v) },

  { key: '求人ボックス', label: '求人ボックス', match: v => ['求人BOX','求人ボックス'].includes(v) },

  { key: '自社求人',     label: '自社（求人）', match: v => ['自社（求人応募）','自社'].includes(v) },

  { key: '自社会員',     label: '自社（会員）', match: v => v === '自社（会員登録）' },

  { key: '自社電話',     label: '自社（電話）', match: v => v === '自社（電話）' },

  { key: '紹介BS',       label: '紹介・BS',     match: v => ['紹介','BS','メイトdeランチ','友人紹介（オーガニック）','ホップ感謝祭'].includes(v) },

  { key: 'エン派遣',     label: 'エン派遣',     match: v => v === 'エン派遣' },

  { key: 'その他',       label: 'その他',       match: v => true }, // 上記以外すべて

];


function getMediaKey(mediaName) {

  const v = String(mediaName || '').trim();

  for (let i = 0; i < MEDIAS.length - 1; i++) {

    if (MEDIAS[i].match(v)) return MEDIAS[i].key;

  }

  return 'その他';

}


let currentSkView = 'media';


function switchSkTab(view) {

  currentSkView = view;

  const tabs = ['media','office'];

  tabs.forEach(t => {

    const btn = document.getElementById('sk-tab-' + t);

    if (t === view) {

      btn.style.borderBottomColor = 'var(--primary)';

      btn.style.color = 'var(--primary)';

      btn.style.fontWeight = '700';

    } else {

      btn.style.borderBottomColor = 'transparent';

      btn.style.color = 'var(--muted)';

      btn.style.fontWeight = 'normal';

    }

  });

  renderShukyaku(view);

}


function syncShukyakuFilters() {

  const y = document.getElementById('sel-year').value;

  const m = document.getElementById('sel-month').value;

  const d = document.getElementById('sel-date').value;

  const ySel = document.getElementById('sk-year');

  const mSel = document.getElementById('sk-month');

  // yearオプションを同期

  const srcSel = document.getElementById('sel-year');

  if (srcSel.options.length > 1 && ySel.options.length <= 1) {

    ySel.innerHTML = srcSel.innerHTML;

  }

  if (y) ySel.value = y;

  if (m) mSel.value = m;

  if (d) document.getElementById('sk-date').value = d;

  buildSkAdvGrid();

  buildSkTargetGrid();

}



// ── 掲載エリア ──────────────────────────────



function buildSkAdvGrid() {

  const grid = document.getElementById('sk-adv-grid');

  if (!grid) return;

  // 共有HTML時はembedDataのcostData、通常時はlocalStorageから読む

  const embedCost = window._EMBEDDED_DATA ? window._EMBEDDED_DATA.costData : null;


  let html = '<div style="overflow-x:auto; width:100%;"><table style="border-collapse:collapse; font-size:10px; white-space:nowrap;">';

  html += '<tr><th style="padding:3px 8px; background:#e8ecf4; border:1px solid var(--border); min-width:60px;">オフィス</th>';

  MEDIAS.forEach(med => {

    html += `<th style="padding:3px 6px; background:#e8ecf4; border:1px solid var(--border); min-width:80px;">${med.label}</th>`;

  });

  html += '</tr>';

  OFFICES.forEach(office => {

    html += `<tr><td style="padding:3px 8px; border:1px solid var(--border); font-weight:700; background:#f4f5f9;">${office}</td>`;

    MEDIAS.forEach(med => {

      const savedVal = embedCost

        ? (embedCost[office]?.[med.key] || '')

        : getSetting(getSkCostKey(office, med.key));

      html += `<td style="padding:2px 4px; border:1px solid var(--border);">

        <input type="text" id="sk-cost-${office}-${med.key}" placeholder="0" value="${savedVal}"

          style="width:72px; padding:2px 4px; border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:3px; font-size:10px; text-align:right;"

          oninput="saveSkCost('${office}','${med.key}',this.value); renderShukyaku(currentSkView)">

      </td>`;

    });

    html += '</tr>';

  });

  html += '</table></div>';

  grid.innerHTML = html;

}


function getSkCostKey(office, mediaKey) {

  const y = document.getElementById('sk-year')?.value || '';

  const m = String(document.getElementById('sk-month')?.value || '').padStart(2,'0');

  return 'sk-cost_' + y + '-' + m + '_' + office + '_' + mediaKey;

}


function saveSkCost(office, mediaKey, value) {

  setSetting(getSkCostKey(office, mediaKey), value);

}


function getSkCost(office, mediaKey) {

  const el = document.getElementById('sk-cost-' + office + '-' + mediaKey);

  if (el) return parseFloat(el.value.replace(/,/g,'')) || 0;

  // elがない場合はlocalStorage（月別）から

  return parseFloat(getSetting(getSkCostKey(office, mediaKey))) || 0;

}


// ══════════════════════════════════════════

// 媒体別 目標有効応募（拠点合算 × 月で1値）

// ══════════════════════════════════════════

function getSkTargetMonthKey(mediaKey) {

  const y = document.getElementById('sk-year')?.value || '';

  const m = String(document.getElementById('sk-month')?.value || '').padStart(2,'0');

  return 'sk-target_' + y + '-' + m + '_' + mediaKey;

}


function saveSkTarget(mediaKey, value) {

  setSetting(getSkTargetMonthKey(mediaKey), value);

}


function getSkMediaTarget(mediaKey) {

  if (mediaKey === 'ALL') {

    // ALL列 = 11媒体の目標値合計

    return MEDIAS.reduce((sum, med) => {

      const el = document.getElementById('sk-target-' + med.key);

      const v = el ? parseFloat(String(el.value).replace(/,/g,'')) : parseFloat(getSetting(getSkTargetMonthKey(med.key)));

      return sum + (v || 0);

    }, 0);

  }

  const el = document.getElementById('sk-target-' + mediaKey);

  if (el) return parseFloat(String(el.value).replace(/,/g,'')) || 0;

  return parseFloat(getSetting(getSkTargetMonthKey(mediaKey))) || 0;

}


function buildSkTargetGrid() {

  const grid = document.getElementById('sk-target-grid');

  if (!grid) return;

  const y = document.getElementById('sk-year')?.value || '';

  const m = document.getElementById('sk-month')?.value || '';

  const label = (y && m) ? y + '年' + m + '月の媒体別 目標有効応募' : '媒体別 目標有効応募（月を選択すると月別保存）';

  const sumEl = document.getElementById('sk-target-bar-summary');

  if (sumEl) sumEl.textContent = label;


  let html = '<div style="overflow-x:auto; width:100%;"><table style="border-collapse:collapse; font-size:10px; white-space:nowrap;"><tbody><tr>';

  MEDIAS.forEach(med => {

    html += `<th style="padding:3px 6px; background:#fff3d4; border:1px solid var(--border); min-width:80px;">${med.label}</th>`;

  });

  html += '</tr><tr>';

  MEDIAS.forEach(med => {

    const savedVal = getSetting(getSkTargetMonthKey(med.key)) || '';

    html += `<td style="padding:2px 4px; border:1px solid var(--border);">

      <input type="text" id="sk-target-${med.key}" placeholder="0" value="${savedVal}"

        style="width:72px; padding:2px 4px; border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:3px; font-size:10px; text-align:right;"

        oninput="saveSkTarget('${med.key}', this.value); renderShukyaku(currentSkView)">

    </td>`;

  });

  html += '</tr></tbody></table></div>';

  grid.innerHTML = html;

}


// 達成率を文字列化（NaNや空は「—」）

function skAchvText(rate) {

  if (rate === null || rate === undefined || isNaN(rate)) return '—';

  return rate.toFixed(1) + '%';

}


function createSkMetrics() {

  return { cost: 0, target: 0, total: 0, valid: 0, contacted: 0,

    validByWeekday: [0,0,0,0,0,0,0],

    selTarget: 0, rA: 0, rB: 0, rC: 0,

    set: 0, impl: 0, dec: 0, start: 0 };

}


// SkMetrics の加算ヘルパー（配列フィールドも要素ごとに加算）

function addSkMetrics(target, source) {

  for (const k in source) {

    if (Array.isArray(source[k])) {

      if (!Array.isArray(target[k])) target[k] = source[k].map(() => 0);

      for (let i = 0; i < source[k].length; i++) {

        target[k][i] = (target[k][i] || 0) + (source[k][i] || 0);

      }

    } else {

      target[k] = (target[k] || 0) + (source[k] || 0);

    }

  }

}


// 基準日R = 最新応募日（統合ツール優先・最下行とは限らないのでmaxで取得）

function getLatestAppDate() {

  let latest = null;

  const scan = (rows, keys) => {

    (rows || []).forEach(r => {

      let v = '';

      for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) { if (r[keys[i]]) { v = r[keys[i]]; break; } }

      const d = parseDate(v);

      if (d && (!latest || d > latest)) latest = d;

    });

  };

  scan(state.medRows, ['応募日', '（応募内容）応募日']);

  if (!latest) scan(state.mcgRawRows, ['応募日']);

  return latest;

}


// 当月内 / 2ヶ月以内 の応募日レンジ（締め＝R前日）

function getCohortRanges() {

  const R = getLatestAppDate();

  if (!R) return null;

  const end      = new Date(R.getFullYear(), R.getMonth(), R.getDate() - 1, 23, 59, 59, 999); // R前日

  const curStart = new Date(R.getFullYear(), R.getMonth(), 1, 0, 0, 0, 0);                     // 当月1日

  const twoStart = new Date(R.getFullYear(), R.getMonth() - 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0);                 // 前月1日

  return { R, end, curStart, twoStart };

}


// 日付として妥当か（'0'/'1'等のゴミを除外。設定日等の「入力あり」判定に使用）

function isRealDate(v) { return /^\d{4}[\/\-]\d{1,2}[\/\-]\d{1,2}/.test((v || '').trim()); }


function computeShukyaku(year, month, baseDate) {

  const data = {};

  OFFICES.forEach(o => {

    data[o] = {};

    MEDIAS.forEach(med => { data[o][med.key] = createSkMetrics(); });

  });

  OFFICES.forEach(o => {

    MEDIAS.forEach(med => { data[o][med.key].cost = getSkCost(o, med.key); });

  });

  const validPhones = buildValidPhones(year, month);

  const medFiltered = (state.medRows || []).filter(r => {

    const d = parseDate(r['応募日'] || r['（応募内容）応募日'] || '');

    if (!d) return false;

    if (year && d.getFullYear() !== year) return false;

    if (month && d.getMonth() + 1 !== month) return false;

    return true;

  });

  medFiltered.forEach(r => {

    const office = normalizeOffice(r['対応オフィス'] || r['拠点'] || '');

    if (!OFFICES.includes(office)) return;

    const mediaKey = getMediaKey(r['媒体']);

    data[office][mediaKey].total++;

    // 有効応募：統合ツールCSVの「有効応募」列が "1" の行をカウント

    if (String(r['有効応募'] || '').trim() === '1') {

      data[office][mediaKey].valid++;

      // 曜日別にもカウント（着地見込算出用）

      const dRow = parseDate(r['応募日'] || r['（応募内容）応募日'] || '');

      if (dRow) data[office][mediaKey].validByWeekday[dRow.getDay()]++;

    }

  });

  const counted = new Set();

  const jinsenCounted = new Set();

  const contactCounted = new Set();

  (state.mcgRows || []).forEach(mcg => {

    const appDate = parseDate(mcg['応募日'] || '');

    if (!appDate) return;

    if (year && appDate.getFullYear() !== year) return;

    if (month && appDate.getMonth() + 1 !== month) return;

    const office = String(mcg['所属'] || '').trim();

    if (!OFFICES.includes(office)) return;

    const mediaKey = getMediaKey(mcg['応募媒体']);

    const phone = normalizePhone(String(mcg['電話番号'] || ''));


    // ── 人選集計（validPhones フィルタ前、所属×媒体×電話でdedup）──

    const jinsenKey = office + '|' + mediaKey + '|' + phone;

    if (!phone || !jinsenCounted.has(jinsenKey)) {

      if (phone) jinsenCounted.add(jinsenKey);

      const jinsenStr = String(mcg['人選ｽﾃｰﾀｽ'] || '').trim();

      let jinsenRank = null;

      if (jinsenStr.indexOf('A人選') === 0)      jinsenRank = 'A';

      else if (jinsenStr.indexOf('B人選') === 0) jinsenRank = 'B';

      else if (jinsenStr.indexOf('C人選') === 0) jinsenRank = 'C';

      const mJ = data[office][mediaKey];

      if (jinsenRank === 'A' || jinsenRank === 'B') mJ.selTarget++;

      if (jinsenRank === 'A') mJ.rA++;

      else if (jinsenRank === 'B') mJ.rB++;

      else if (jinsenRank === 'C') mJ.rC++;

    }


    // ── 接触集計（有効応募フィルタなし、所属×媒体×電話でdedup）──

    const contactKey = office + '|' + mediaKey + '|' + phone;

    if (!phone || !contactCounted.has(contactKey)) {

      if (phone) contactCounted.add(contactKey);

      const 接触RawC = mcg['接触ステータス'] || '';

      const contactedC = 接触RawC.trim() !== '' && !接触RawC.split('\n').every(s => s.trim() === '未接触' || s.trim() === '');

      if (contactedC) data[office][mediaKey].contacted++;

    }


    // ── 以下、validPhonesフィルタあり（ファネル用）──

    if (!phone || !validPhones.has(phone)) return;

    const dedupKey = office + '|' + mediaKey + '|' + phone;

    if (counted.has(dedupKey)) return;

    counted.add(dedupKey);

    const m = data[office][mediaKey];

    // ※ m.valid は統合ツール側で集計済み

    const dateOK = (key) => { const d = parseDate(mcg[key]); return d && (baseDate ? d <= baseDate : true); };

    if (dateOK('設定日（新規）')) m.set++;

    // 実施：実施キャンセル以外 × 今日以前

    const implDate = parseDate(mcg['実施日（新規）']);

    const todaySk = new Date(); todaySk.setHours(23,59,59,999);

    if (implDate

      && implDate <= todaySk

      && (baseDate ? implDate <= baseDate : true)

      && (mcg['★新規就業ステータス'] || '').trim() !== '実施キャンセル')

      m.impl++;

    if (dateOK('決定日（新規）')) m.dec++;

    if (dateOK('開始日（新規）')) m.start++;

  });

  return data;

}


function renderShukyaku(view) {

  if (!state.medRows && !state.mcgRows && !window._PRECOMPUTED_SK) return;

  const wrap = document.getElementById('sk-table-wrap');

  if (!wrap) return;

  const year  = parseInt(document.getElementById('sk-year').value) || null;

  const month = parseInt(document.getElementById('sk-month').value) || null;

  const baseDateVal = document.getElementById('sk-date').value;

  const baseDate = baseDateVal ? new Date(baseDateVal + 'T23:59:59') : null;

  const ym = (year && month) ? `${year}年${month}月` : '（月を選択）';


  let data;

  if (window._PRECOMPUTED_SK) {

    // 集計済みデータを使用（コスト反映）

    data = window._PRECOMPUTED_SK;

    OFFICES.forEach(o => {

      MEDIAS.forEach(med => {

        if (data[o] && data[o][med.key]) data[o][med.key].cost = getSkCost(o, med.key);

      });

    });

  } else {

    data = computeShukyaku(year, month, baseDate);

  }


  if (view === 'media') {

    renderSkMediaView(data, ym, wrap);

  } else {

    renderSkOfficeView(data, ym, wrap);

  }

}


// ── 媒体別ビュー（列：媒体、行：オフィス）──

function renderSkMediaView(data, ym, wrap) {

  const fmtC = n => n ? '¥' + Math.round(n).toLocaleString() : '';

  const fmtCpa = (cost, cnt) => (cost && cnt) ? '¥' + Math.round(cost/cnt).toLocaleString() : '';

  const fmtR = (n, d) => d ? (n/d*100).toFixed(1)+'%' : '';


  // 合計列を追加

  const mediaCols = [...MEDIAS, { key: 'ALL', label: 'ALL合計' }];


  let html = '<table><thead><tr>';

  html += `<th class="lbl-cell th-space">${ym}</th>`;

  mediaCols.forEach(med => {

    const isTot = med.key === 'ALL';

    html += `<th class="${isTot ? 'th-total' : 'th-office'}" style="min-width:80px;">${med.label}</th>`;

  });

  html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';


  // オフィスごとの行 + 合計行

  const allOffices = ['ALL合計', ...OFFICES];

  allOffices.forEach(office => {

    const isTotal = office === 'ALL合計';

    const rowCls = isTotal ? 'style="background:#e8ecf4;"' : '';


    // 各メディアのメトリクスを集計

    const getM = (mediaKey) => {

      if (isTotal) {

        // 全オフィスの合計

        const m = createSkMetrics();

        OFFICES.forEach(o => {

          const src = mediaKey === 'ALL' ? MEDIAS.reduce((acc, med) => {

            const s = data[o][med.key];

            addSkMetrics(acc, s);

            return acc;

          }, createSkMetrics()) : data[o][mediaKey];

          addSkMetrics(m, src);

        });

        return m;

      } else {

        if (mediaKey === 'ALL') {

          return MEDIAS.reduce((acc, med) => {

            const s = data[office][med.key];

            addSkMetrics(acc, s);

            return acc;

          }, createSkMetrics());

        }

        return data[office][mediaKey];

      }

    };


    // 媒体ごとの 4目標指標は ALL合計 セクションでのみ表示

    const baseDateVal_sk = document.getElementById('sk-date')?.value || '';

    const baseDate_sk = baseDateVal_sk ? new Date(baseDateVal_sk + 'T23:59:59') : null;

    const _skYear = parseInt(document.getElementById('sk-year').value) || null;

    const _skMonth = parseInt(document.getElementById('sk-month').value) || null;

    const targetRows = isTotal ? [

      ['🎯目標有効応募', (m, mk) => {

        const tgt = getSkMediaTarget(mk);

        return tgt > 0 ? fmt(tgt) : '—';

      }, 'lbl-main', 'target'],

      ['（日割り達成率）', (m, mk) => {

        const tgt = getSkMediaTarget(mk);

        const fc = computeForecastForOffice(m, tgt, _skYear, _skMonth, baseDate_sk);

        return fc ? skAchvText(fc.daily) : '—';

      }, 'lbl-sub', 'daily'],

      ['📈着地見込', (m, mk) => {

        const tgt = getSkMediaTarget(mk);

        const fc = computeForecastForOffice(m, tgt, _skYear, _skMonth, baseDate_sk);

        return fc ? fmt(fc.forecast) : '—';

      }, 'lbl-main', 'forecast'],

      ['（見込達成率）', (m, mk) => {

        const tgt = getSkMediaTarget(mk);

        const fc = computeForecastForOffice(m, tgt, _skYear, _skMonth, baseDate_sk);

        return fc ? skAchvText(fc.forecastRate) : '—';

      }, 'lbl-sub', 'fcrate'],

    ] : [];


    const metrics = [

      ['集客費用', m => fmtC(m.cost), 'lbl-sub'],

      ['総応募', m => fmt(m.total), 'lbl-main'],

      ['有効応募', m => fmt(m.valid), 'lbl-star'],

      ['', m => fmtR(m.valid, m.total), 'lbl-sub'],

      ...targetRows,

      ['接触数', m => fmt(m.contacted), 'lbl-main'],

      ['', m => fmtR(m.contacted, m.valid), 'lbl-sub'],

      ['人選対象数', m => fmt(m.selTarget), 'lbl-star'],

      ['　★★★★', m => fmt(m.rA), 'lbl-sub2'],

      ['　★★★☆', m => fmt(m.rB), 'lbl-sub2'],

      ['　★★☆☆', m => fmt(m.rC), 'lbl-sub2'],

      ['設定数', m => fmt(m.set), 'lbl-main'],

      ['実施数', m => fmt(m.impl), 'lbl-main'],

      ['決定数', m => fmt(m.dec), 'lbl-main'],

      ['開始数', m => fmt(m.start), 'lbl-main'],

      ['総応募単価', m => fmtCpa(m.cost, m.total), 'lbl-sub'],

      ['有効応募単価', m => fmtCpa(m.cost, m.valid), 'lbl-sub'],

      ['設定単価', m => fmtCpa(m.cost, m.set), 'lbl-sub'],

      ['開始単価', m => fmtCpa(m.cost, m.start), 'lbl-sub'],

    ];


    // オフィス名ヘッダー行（合計以外は媒体名も表示）

    if (isTotal) {

      html += `<tr><td class="lbl-cell lbl-main" colspan="${mediaCols.length + 1}" style="background:#334e7a; color:#fff; padding:4px 8px; font-weight:700;">合計</td></tr>`;

    } else {

      html += `<tr><td class="lbl-cell lbl-main" style="background:var(--primary); color:#fff; padding:4px 8px; font-weight:700;">${office}</td>`;

      mediaCols.forEach(med => {

        html += `<td style="background:var(--primary); color:#fff; font-size:9px; text-align:center; padding:2px 4px; border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.2);">${med.label}</td>`;

      });

      html += '</tr>';

    }


    metrics.forEach((entry) => {

      const [label, fn, cls, marker] = entry;

      // target系のラベルは黄色背景を適用

      const labelStyle = marker ? 'background:#fff3d4;' : '';

      html += `<tr ${rowCls}><td class="lbl-cell ${cls}" style="padding-left:${label?'7px':'20px'};${labelStyle}">${label || '（割合）'}</td>`;

      mediaCols.forEach(med => {

        const m = getM(med.key);

        const isTotCol = med.key === 'ALL';

        const cellVal = fn(m, med.key);

        // 達成率セルの色分け（daily/fcrate）

        let cellExtraCls = '';

        if (marker === 'daily' || marker === 'fcrate') {

          const tgt = getSkMediaTarget(med.key);

          const fc = computeForecastForOffice(m, tgt, _skYear, _skMonth, baseDate_sk);

          if (fc) {

            const rate = marker === 'daily' ? fc.daily : fc.forecastRate;

            if (rate !== null && !isNaN(rate)) {

              cellExtraCls = rate >= 100 ? ' lt-good' : (rate >= 80 ? ' lt-warn' : ' lt-bad');

            }

          }

        }

        html += `<td class="${isTotCol ? 'val-total' : 'val-main'}${cellExtraCls}">${cellVal}</td>`;

      });

      html += '</tr>';

    });

  });


  html += '</tbody></table>';

  wrap.innerHTML = html;

}


// ── オフィス別ビュー（列：オフィス、行：媒体）──

function renderSkOfficeView(data, ym, wrap) {

  const fmtC = n => n ? '¥' + Math.round(n).toLocaleString() : '';

  const fmtCpa = (cost, cnt) => (cost && cnt) ? '¥' + Math.round(cost/cnt).toLocaleString() : '';

  const fmtR = (n, d) => d ? (n/d*100).toFixed(1)+'%' : '';


  const officeCols = [...OFFICES, 'ALL合計'];


  let html = '<table><thead><tr>';

  html += `<th class="lbl-cell th-space">${ym}</th>`;

  officeCols.forEach(o => {

    const isTot = o === 'ALL合計';

    html += `<th class="${isTot ? 'th-total' : 'th-office'}">${isTot ? '合計' : o}</th>`;

  });

  html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';


  const getOfficeM = (office, mediaKey) => {

    if (mediaKey === 'ALL_ROW') {

      // 全媒体合計

      const m = createSkMetrics();

      if (office === 'ALL合計') {

        OFFICES.forEach(o => {

          MEDIAS.forEach(med => {

            const s = data[o][med.key];

            Object.keys(m).forEach(k => { m[k] += s[k]; });

          });

        });

      } else {

        MEDIAS.forEach(med => {

          const s = data[office][med.key];

          Object.keys(m).forEach(k => { m[k] += s[k]; });

        });

      }

      return m;

    }

    if (office === 'ALL合計') {

      const m = createSkMetrics();

      OFFICES.forEach(o => {

        const s = data[o][mediaKey];

        Object.keys(m).forEach(k => { m[k] += s[k]; });

      });

      return m;

    }

    return data[office][mediaKey];

  };


  // 媒体ごとのセクション（合計→各媒体の順）

  const allMediaRows = [{ key: 'ALL_ROW', label: '合計' }, ...MEDIAS];


  // 着地見込算出用の年月・基準日

  const _skoYear = parseInt(document.getElementById('sk-year').value) || null;

  const _skoMonth = parseInt(document.getElementById('sk-month').value) || null;

  const _skoBaseDateVal = document.getElementById('sk-date')?.value || '';

  const _skoBaseDate = _skoBaseDateVal ? new Date(_skoBaseDateVal + 'T23:59:59') : null;


  allMediaRows.forEach(med => {

    // 媒体名バー（合計以外はオフィス名も表示）

    if (med.key === 'ALL_ROW') {

      html += `<tr><td class="lbl-cell lbl-main" colspan="${officeCols.length + 1}" style="background:#334e7a; color:#fff; padding:4px 8px;">合計</td></tr>`;

    } else {

      html += `<tr><td class="lbl-cell lbl-main" style="background:var(--primary-light); color:#fff; padding:4px 8px;">${med.label}</td>`;

      officeCols.forEach(o => {

        const isTot = o === 'ALL合計';

        html += `<td style="background:${isTot ? '#334e7a' : 'var(--primary-light)'}; color:#fff; font-size:9px; text-align:center; padding:2px 4px; border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.2);">${isTot ? '合計' : o}</td>`;

      });

      html += '</tr>';

    }


    // 拠点目標を取得（合計セクションのみ使用）

    const getTargetForOfficeKey = (officeKey) => {

      if (officeKey === 'ALL合計') {

        return OFFICES.reduce((sum, o) => sum + getOfficeTarget(o), 0);

      }

      return getOfficeTarget(officeKey);

    };


    // 合計セクション（全媒体合算）のみ、人材DB拠点別目標を使った4行を追加

    const targetRows = (med.key === 'ALL_ROW') ? [

      ['🎯目標有効応募', (m, oKey) => {

        const tgt = getTargetForOfficeKey(oKey);

        return tgt > 0 ? fmt(tgt) : '—';

      }, 'lbl-main', 'target'],

      ['（日割り達成率）', (m, oKey) => {

        const tgt = getTargetForOfficeKey(oKey);

        const fc = computeForecastForOffice(m, tgt, _skoYear, _skoMonth, _skoBaseDate);

        return fc ? skAchvText(fc.daily) : '—';

      }, 'lbl-sub', 'daily'],

      ['📈着地見込', (m, oKey) => {

        const tgt = getTargetForOfficeKey(oKey);

        const fc = computeForecastForOffice(m, tgt, _skoYear, _skoMonth, _skoBaseDate);

        return fc ? fmt(fc.forecast) : '—';

      }, 'lbl-main', 'forecast'],

      ['（見込達成率）', (m, oKey) => {

        const tgt = getTargetForOfficeKey(oKey);

        const fc = computeForecastForOffice(m, tgt, _skoYear, _skoMonth, _skoBaseDate);

        return fc ? skAchvText(fc.forecastRate) : '—';

      }, 'lbl-sub', 'fcrate'],

    ] : [];


    const metrics = [

      ['集客費用', m => fmtC(m.cost), 'lbl-sub'],

      ['総応募', m => fmt(m.total), 'lbl-main'],

      ['有効応募', m => fmt(m.valid), 'lbl-star'],

      ['', m => fmtR(m.valid, m.total), 'lbl-sub'],

      ...targetRows,

      ['接触数', m => fmt(m.contacted), 'lbl-main'],

      ['人選対象数', m => fmt(m.selTarget), 'lbl-star'],

      ['　★★★★', m => fmt(m.rA), 'lbl-sub2'],

      ['　★★★☆', m => fmt(m.rB), 'lbl-sub2'],

      ['　★★☆☆', m => fmt(m.rC), 'lbl-sub2'],

      ['設定数', m => fmt(m.set), 'lbl-main'],

      ['実施数', m => fmt(m.impl), 'lbl-main'],

      ['決定数', m => fmt(m.dec), 'lbl-main'],

      ['開始数', m => fmt(m.start), 'lbl-main'],

      ['総応募単価', m => fmtCpa(m.cost, m.total), 'lbl-sub'],

      ['有効応募単価', m => fmtCpa(m.cost, m.valid), 'lbl-sub'],

      ['開始単価', m => fmtCpa(m.cost, m.start), 'lbl-sub'],

    ];


    metrics.forEach((entry) => {

      const [label, fn, cls, marker] = entry;

      const labelStyle = marker ? 'background:#fff3d4;' : '';

      html += `<tr><td class="lbl-cell ${cls}" style="padding-left:${label?'7px':'20px'};${labelStyle}">${label || '（割合）'}</td>`;

      officeCols.forEach(o => {

        const m = getOfficeM(o, med.key);

        const isTot = o === 'ALL合計';

        const cellVal = fn(m, o);

        // 達成率セル色分け

        let cellExtraCls = '';

        if (marker === 'daily' || marker === 'fcrate') {

          const tgt = getTargetForOfficeKey(o);

          const fc = computeForecastForOffice(m, tgt, _skoYear, _skoMonth, _skoBaseDate);

          if (fc) {

            const rate = marker === 'daily' ? fc.daily : fc.forecastRate;

            if (rate !== null && !isNaN(rate)) {

              cellExtraCls = rate >= 100 ? ' lt-good' : (rate >= 80 ? ' lt-warn' : ' lt-bad');

            }

          }

        }

        html += `<td class="${isTot ? 'val-total' : 'val-main'}${cellExtraCls}">${cellVal}</td>`;

      });

      html += '</tr>';

    });

  });


  html += '</tbody></table>';

  wrap.innerHTML = html;

}



// ── ページ切り替え ──

function showPage(page, el) {

  document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar-item').forEach(e => e.classList.remove('active'));

  if (el) el.classList.add('active');


  // 全ページ非表示

  ['jinzai-page', 'page-pref', 'page-shukyaku', 'page-nicji', 'page-nicsb', 'page-tsuuki'].forEach(id => {

    const p = document.getElementById(id);

    if (p) p.style.display = 'none';

  });


  if (page === 'tsuuki') {

    document.getElementById('page-title').textContent = '通期DB';

    document.getElementById('page-tsuuki').style.display = 'block';

    renderTsuuki();

  } else if (page === 'nicsb') {

    document.getElementById('page-title').textContent = '日次スコアボード';

    document.getElementById('page-nicsb').style.display = 'block';

    renderNicSB();

  } else if (page === 'nicji') {

    document.getElementById('page-title').textContent = '日次DB';

    document.getElementById('page-nicji').style.display = 'block';

    if ((state.mcgRows || []).length > 0) {

      renderNicji();

    } else {

      setTimeout(renderNicji, 1500);

    }

  } else if (page === 'jinzai') {

    document.getElementById('page-title').textContent = '人材ダッシュボード';

    document.getElementById('jinzai-page').style.display = 'block';

  } else if (page === 'pref') {

    document.getElementById('page-title').textContent = '都道府県分析';

    document.getElementById('page-pref').style.display = 'block';

    syncPrefFilters();

    renderPref();

  } else if (page === 'shukyaku') {

    document.getElementById('page-title').textContent = '集客ダッシュボード';

    document.getElementById('page-shukyaku').style.display = 'block';

    buildSkAdvGrid();

    syncShukyakuFilters();

    renderShukyaku('media');

  } else if (filterBy === 'media') {

    MEDIAS.forEach(m => { const opt = document.createElement('option'); opt.value = m.key; opt.textContent = m.label; filterVal.appendChild(opt); });

  }

}



function normalizePref(raw) {

  if (!raw || raw === '0' || raw === 0) return null;

  const s = String(raw).trim();

  if (!s || s === '0') return null;


  // 全管轄都道府県リスト

  const allPrefs = Object.values(PREF_MAP).flatMap(({ prefs }) => prefs);


  // 完全一致

  if (allPrefs.includes(s)) return s;


  // 先頭3文字で管轄都道府県に一致（長い住所対応）

  for (const p of allPrefs) {

    if (s.startsWith(p.slice(0, 3))) return p;

  }


  // 末尾に都道府県がない場合は補完（「神奈川」→「神奈川県」など）

  for (const suffix of ['都', '道', '府', '県']) {

    if (allPrefs.includes(s + suffix)) return s + suffix;

  }


  // 「大阪市」→「大阪府」など市区町村から推測

  for (const p of allPrefs) {

    if (s.includes(p.slice(0, 2))) return p;

  }


  return null;

}


function syncPrefFilters() {

  const y = document.getElementById('sel-year').value;

  const m = document.getElementById('sel-month').value;

  const d = document.getElementById('sel-date').value;

  if (y) document.getElementById('pref-year').value = y;

  if (m) document.getElementById('pref-month').value = m;

  if (d) document.getElementById('pref-date').value = d;


  // pref-yearのオプションを同期

  const srcSel = document.getElementById('sel-year');

  const dstSel = document.getElementById('pref-year');

  if (srcSel.options.length > 1 && dstSel.options.length <= 1) {

    dstSel.innerHTML = srcSel.innerHTML;

    if (y) dstSel.value = y;

  }

}


function createPrefMetrics() {

  return {

    total: 0, valid: 0, contacted: 0,

    selTarget: 0, rA: 0, rB: 0, rC: 0,

    set: 0, rASet: 0, rBSet: 0, rCSet: 0,

    impl: 0, rAImpl: 0, rBImpl: 0, rCImpl: 0,

    dec: 0, rADec: 0, rBDec: 0, rCDec: 0,

    start: 0, rAStart: 0, rBStart: 0, rCStart: 0,

  };

}


function computePref(year, month, baseDate) {

  const D = {};

  for (const { prefs } of Object.values(PREF_MAP)) {

    for (const p of prefs) D[p] = createPrefMetrics();

  }

  Object.keys(PREF_MAP).forEach(office => { D['その他_' + office] = createPrefMetrics(); });


  const prefToOffice = {};

  for (const [office, { prefs }] of Object.entries(PREF_MAP)) {

    for (const p of prefs) { if (!p.startsWith('その他_')) prefToOffice[p] = office; }

  }


  const medFiltered = (state.medRows || []).filter(r => {

    const d = parseDate(r['応募日'] || r['（応募内容）応募日'] || '');

    if (!d) return false;

    if (year && d.getFullYear() !== year) return false;

    if (month && d.getMonth() + 1 !== month) return false;

    return true;

  });


  medFiltered.forEach(r => {

    const pref = normalizePref(r['都道府県名']);

    const isValid = String(r['有効応募'] || '').trim() === '1';

    if (pref && D[pref]) {

      D[pref].total++;

      if (isValid) D[pref].valid++;

    }

    else {

      const office = normalizeOffice(r['対応オフィス'] || r['拠点'] || '');

      const otherKey = 'その他_' + office;

      if (D[otherKey]) {

        D[otherKey].total++;

        if (isValid) D[otherKey].valid++;

      }

    }

  });


  const countedPhones = new Set();

  const jinsenCounted = new Set();

  const contactCountedPref = new Set();

  const validPhones = buildValidPhones(year, month);

  (state.mcgRows || []).forEach(mcg => {

    const appDate = parseDate(mcg['応募日'] || '');

    if (!appDate) return;

    if (year && appDate.getFullYear() !== year) return;

    if (month && appDate.getMonth() + 1 !== month) return;

   const phone = normalizePhone(String(mcg['電話番号'] || ''));


    // ── 人選集計（validPhones フィルタ前、電話でdedup）──

    if (phone && !jinsenCounted.has(phone)) {

      jinsenCounted.add(phone);

      const prefRawTop = normalizePref(mcg['都道府県'] || '');

      let prefTop;

      if (prefRawTop && D[prefRawTop]) { prefTop = prefRawTop; }

      else { prefTop = 'その他_' + String(mcg['所属'] || '').trim(); }

      if (D[prefTop]) {

        const jinsenStrTop = String(mcg['人選ｽﾃｰﾀｽ'] || '').trim();

        let jinsenRankTop = null;

        if (jinsenStrTop.indexOf('A人選') === 0)      jinsenRankTop = 'A';

        else if (jinsenStrTop.indexOf('B人選') === 0) jinsenRankTop = 'B';

        else if (jinsenStrTop.indexOf('C人選') === 0) jinsenRankTop = 'C';

        if (jinsenRankTop === 'A' || jinsenRankTop === 'B') D[prefTop].selTarget++;

        if (jinsenRankTop === 'A') D[prefTop].rA++;

        else if (jinsenRankTop === 'B') D[prefTop].rB++;

        else if (jinsenRankTop === 'C') D[prefTop].rC++;

      }

    }


    // ── 接触集計（有効応募フィルタなし、電話でdedup）──

    if (phone && !contactCountedPref.has(phone)) {

      contactCountedPref.add(phone);

      const prefRawC = normalizePref(mcg['都道府県'] || '');

      let prefC;

      if (prefRawC && D[prefRawC]) { prefC = prefRawC; }

      else { prefC = 'その他_' + String(mcg['所属'] || '').trim(); }

      if (D[prefC]) {

        const 接触RawC = mcg['接触ステータス'] || '';

        const contactedC = 接触RawC.trim() !== '' && !接触RawC.split('\n').every(s => s.trim() === '未接触' || s.trim() === '');

        if (contactedC) D[prefC].contacted++;

      }

    }


    // ── 以下、validPhones フィルタあり（ファネル）──

    if (!phone || !validPhones.has(phone)) return;

    const dedupKey = phone;

    if (countedPhones.has(dedupKey)) return;

    countedPhones.add(dedupKey);

    const prefRaw = normalizePref(mcg['都道府県'] || '');

    let pref;

    if (prefRaw && D[prefRaw]) { pref = prefRaw; }

    else { pref = 'その他_' + String(mcg['所属'] || '').trim(); }

    if (!D[pref]) return;

    // ※ valid は統合ツール側で集計済み

    // rawデータからフラグ計算（Excelなし）

    // 人選ランク：MG CSVの「人選ｽﾃｰﾀｽ」カラムから直接読む

    const jinsenStrPref = String(mcg['人選ｽﾃｰﾀｽ'] || '').trim();

    let rank = null;

    if (jinsenStrPref.indexOf('A人選') === 0)      rank = 'A';

    else if (jinsenStrPref.indexOf('B人選') === 0) rank = 'B';

    else if (jinsenStrPref.indexOf('C人選') === 0) rank = 'C';

    // 人選対象数・ランク内訳は validPhones フィルタ前に上で集計済み

    // （ここでは加算しない。ただし ファネル内訳rASet等 は rank を使って下記で集計）

    const dateOK = (key) => { const d = parseDate(mcg[key]); return d && (baseDate ? d <= baseDate : true); };

    if (dateOK('設定日（新規）')) { D[pref].set++; if(rank==='A')D[pref].rASet++; else if(rank==='B')D[pref].rBSet++; else if(rank==='C')D[pref].rCSet++; }

    if (dateOK('実施日（新規）')) { D[pref].impl++; if(rank==='A')D[pref].rAImpl++; else if(rank==='B')D[pref].rBImpl++; else if(rank==='C')D[pref].rCImpl++; }

    if (dateOK('決定日（新規）')) { D[pref].dec++; if(rank==='A')D[pref].rADec++; else if(rank==='B')D[pref].rBDec++; else if(rank==='C')D[pref].rCDec++; }

    if (dateOK('開始日（新規）')) { D[pref].start++; if(rank==='A')D[pref].rAStart++; else if(rank==='B')D[pref].rBStart++; else if(rank==='C')D[pref].rCStart++; }

  });

  return D;

}


function renderPref() {

  if (!state.medRows && !state.mcgRows && !window._PRECOMPUTED_PREF) return;

  const wrap = document.getElementById('pref-table-wrap');

  if (!wrap) return;


  const year  = parseInt(document.getElementById('pref-year').value) || null;

  const month = parseInt(document.getElementById('pref-month').value) || null;

  const baseDateVal = document.getElementById('pref-date').value;

  const baseDate = baseDateVal ? new Date(baseDateVal + 'T23:59:59') : null;


  const D = window._PRECOMPUTED_PREF || computePref(year, month, baseDate);


  // テーブル生成

  // その他（管轄外都道府県）のメトリクスはループ前に初期化済み


  const ym = (year && month) ? year + '年' + month + '月' : '（月を選択）';

  let html = '<table><thead>';


  // 1段目：オフィス名（colspan）+ その他

  html += `<tr><th class="lbl-cell th-space">${ym}</th>`;

  for (const [office, { prefs }] of Object.entries(PREF_MAP)) {

    html += `<th class="th-office" colspan="${prefs.length}" style="text-align:center;">${office}</th>`;

  }

  html += '</tr>';


  // 2段目：都道府県名 + その他

  html += '<tr><th class="lbl-cell th-space"></th>';

  for (const { prefs } of Object.values(PREF_MAP)) {

    for (const p of prefs) {

      const label = p.startsWith('その他_') ? 'その他' : p.replace(/[都道府県]$/, '');

      const bg = p.startsWith('その他_') ? '#4a6fa5' : '#2d5186';

      html += `<th class="th-office" style="font-size:9px; background:${bg};">${label}</th>`;

    }

  }

  html += '</tr></thead><tbody>';


  const allPrefs = Object.values(PREF_MAP).flatMap(({ prefs }) => prefs);


  const addRow = (label, cls, fn, subFn, subLabel) => {

    html += `<tr><td class="lbl-cell ${cls}">${label}</td>`;

    allPrefs.forEach(p => {

      const m = D[p];

      const isOther = p.startsWith('その他_');

      html += `<td class="${isOther ? 'val-sub' : 'val-main'}">${fn(m)}</td>`;

    });

    html += '</tr>';

    if (subFn) {

      html += `<tr><td class="lbl-cell lbl-sub">${subLabel}</td>`;

      allPrefs.forEach(p => { html += subFn(D[p]); });

      html += '</tr>';

    }

  };


  const fmtR = (num, den, warn) => {

    if (!den) return '<td class="val-sub"></td>';

    const r = num / den * 100;

    const wc = warn && r < warn ? ' warn-red' : '';

    return `<td class="val-sub${wc}">${r.toFixed(1)}%</td>`;

  };


  addRow('総応募', 'lbl-main', m => fmt(m.total));

  addRow('有効応募', 'lbl-main lbl-star', m => fmt(m.valid),

    m => fmtR(m.valid, m.total, 70), '（有効率）');

  addRow('接触数', 'lbl-main', m => fmt(m.contacted),

    m => fmtR(m.contacted, m.valid, 90), '（接触率）');

  addRow('設定数', 'lbl-main', m => fmt(m.set),

    m => fmtR(m.set, m.valid, null), '（設定率）');

  addRow('実施数', 'lbl-main', m => fmt(m.impl),

    m => fmtR(m.impl, m.set, null), '（実施率）');

  addRow('決定数', 'lbl-main', m => fmt(m.dec),

    m => fmtR(m.dec, m.impl, null), '（決定率）');

  addRow('開始数', 'lbl-main', m => fmt(m.start),

    m => fmtR(m.start, m.selTarget, null), '（開始率 / 人選対象比）');


  // ── 人選・ファネルセクション ──

  // セクションヘッダー

  html += `<tr class="sec-header"><td class="lbl-cell">🎯 人選・ファネル</td>${allPrefs.map(()=>'<td></td>').join('')}</tr>`;


  const addRankRows = (getTotal, getA, getB, getC) => {

    const ranks = [['★★★★', getA], ['★★★☆', getB], ['★★☆☆', getC]];

    return ranks.map(([lbl, getFn]) => {

      let row = `<tr><td class="lbl-cell lbl-sub2">　${lbl}</td>`;

      allPrefs.forEach(p => {

        row += `<td class="val-sub2">${fmt(getFn(D[p]))}</td>`;

      });

      return row + '</tr>';

    }).join('');

  };


  // 人選対象数（接触済み）

  addRow('人選対象数', 'lbl-main lbl-star', m => fmt(m.selTarget),

    m => fmtR(m.selTarget, m.valid, null), '（有効応募比）');

  html += addRankRows(m=>m.selTarget, m=>m.rA, m=>m.rB, m=>m.rC);


  // 設定数

  addRow('設定数', 'lbl-main', m => fmt(m.set),

    m => fmtR(m.set, m.valid, null), '（設定率）');

  html += addRankRows(m=>m.set, m=>m.rASet, m=>m.rBSet, m=>m.rCSet);


  // 実施数

  addRow('実施数', 'lbl-main', m => fmt(m.impl),

    m => fmtR(m.impl, m.set, null), '（実施率）');

  html += addRankRows(m=>m.impl, m=>m.rAImpl, m=>m.rBImpl, m=>m.rCImpl);


  // 決定数

  addRow('決定数', 'lbl-main', m => fmt(m.dec),

    m => fmtR(m.dec, m.impl, null), '（決定率）');

  html += addRankRows(m=>m.dec, m=>m.rADec, m=>m.rBDec, m=>m.rCDec);


  // 開始数

  addRow('開始数', 'lbl-main', m => fmt(m.start),

    m => fmtR(m.start, m.selTarget, null), '（開始率 / 人選対象比）');

  html += addRankRows(m=>m.start, m=>m.rAStart, m=>m.rBStart, m=>m.rCStart);


  html += '</tbody></table>';

  wrap.innerHTML = html;

}



// MCG未入力リスト描画

function renderUnregisteredList() {

  const list = state.mcgUnregisteredList || [];

  const panel = document.getElementById('unreg-panel');

  const tbody = document.getElementById('unreg-body');

  if (!list.length) { panel.style.display = 'none'; return; }

  panel.style.display = 'block';

  tbody.innerHTML = list.map((r, i) => {

    const bg = i % 2 === 0 ? '#fff' : '#fdf8f8';

    return `<tr style="background:${bg};">

      <td style="padding:5px 12px; border:1px solid #f5c6c2;">${r.date.substring(0,10)}</td>

      <td style="padding:5px 12px; border:1px solid #f5c6c2; font-weight:700;">${r.name}</td>

      <td style="padding:5px 12px; border:1px solid #f5c6c2;">${r.media}</td>

      <td style="padding:5px 12px; border:1px solid #f5c6c2;">${r.office}</td>

      <td style="padding:5px 12px; border:1px solid #f5c6c2;">${r.tel}</td>

    </tr>`;

  }).join('');

}


// CSVダウンロード

function downloadUnreg() {

  const list = state.mcgUnregisteredList || [];

  const bom = '\uFEFF';

  const header = '応募日,氏名,媒体,オフィス,電話番号\n';

  const rows = list.map(r =>

    [r.date.substring(0,10), r.name, r.media, r.office, r.tel]

    .map(v => `"${v}"`).join(',')

  ).join('\n');

  const blob = new Blob([bom + header + rows], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });

  const a = document.createElement('a');

  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

  a.download = 'MCG未入力リスト.csv';

  a.click();

}

      </td><td style="padding:2px 4px; border:1px solid var(--border);">
